- **si-wiki-fetch**: Use when you need authoritative Spirit Island mechanical data (spirits, unique cards, innates, special rules) — NEVER write mechanics from memory or from LLM-summarized WebFetch. Use before authoring or revising any spirit chapter, card reference, or fact-checking a mechanical claim. ([bfowle/spirit-island](https://github.com/bfowle/spirit-island/tree/HEAD/skills/si-wiki-fetch))
